Source finding context: 외부 시스템이 운영 권위를 유지하더라도 DelinquencyStatus 또는 CreditImpairmentStatus 같은 참조 엔티티를 온톨로지에 포함한다. 상태값, status_as_of, source_system, lifecycle transition, reporting_bucket, relationship to Exposure/Borrower를 최소 범위로 정의해 공유 분류 기준에는 포함되게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: 여신 리스크 관리 개념 모델과 리스크 엔진/보고 시스템의 공유 분류 기준 Source finding context: 보고서나 엔진이 익스포저의 연체·부실 여부를 등급, 한도, 리스크 성향 위반과 함께 분류·집계해야 하는 경우 Source finding context: 연체/부실 상태가 별도 시스템에 있다는 사실만으로는 공유 온톨로지에서 상태 의미와 lifecycle을 참조할 수 없어 핵심 리스크 상태 범위가 단절된다. Source finding context: 운영 소유 시스템의 범위와 공유 개념 기준의 범위를 동일시해 외부 소유 상태를 참조 개념으로도 포함하지 않았다. Source finding context: 대상은 연체/부실 상태를 별도 시스템에서 다룬다는 이유로 온톨로지 범위 밖이라고 선언한다. Source finding context: entities와 relations에는 Borrower 또는 Exposure의 연체·부실 상태나 상태 전환 개념이 없다. Source finding context: 따라서 공유 리스크 분류 기준은 익스포저 lifecycle의 부실화 이후 구간을 표현하지 못한다.
  - affected purpose: 여신 리스크 관리 개념 모델과 리스크 엔진/보고 시스템의 공유 분류 기준.
  - failure condition: 보고서나 엔진이 익스포저의 연체·부실 여부를 등급, 한도, 리스크 성향 위반과 함께 분류·집계해야 하는 경우.
  - impact: 연체/부실 상태가 별도 시스템에 있다는 사실만으로는 공유 온톨로지에서 상태 의미와 lifecycle을 참조할 수 없어 핵심 리스크 상태 범위가 단절된다.
  - root hypothesis: 운영 소유 시스템의 범위와 공유 개념 기준의 범위를 동일시해 externally owned delinquency/default status를 reference concept으로도 포함하지 않았다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004`, `Embedded Materialized Input: header purpose statement`, `Embedded Materialized Input: notes excluding delinquency/default`, `Embedded Materialized Input: entities list lacks delinquency/default status`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-008`, `finding-008.cause-001`, `finding-008.cause-002`, `Embedded Materialized Input: entities list`, `Embedded Materialized Input: relations list`, `finding-008.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.coverage`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-008`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.logic`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-008`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-008.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: coverage
  - action candidates: follow_up
  - materiality: 대상 온톨로지는 여신 리스크 관리 개념 모델이자 리스크 엔진/보고 시스템의 공유 분류 기준을 목적으로 한다. 보고서나 엔진이 익스포저의 연체·부실 여부를 등급, 한도, 리스크 성향 위반과 함께 분류·집계해야 할 때, 연체/default 상태 의미와 lifecycle을 참조할 shared concept이 없으면 핵심 리스크 상태 범위가 단절되어 선언된 목적을 약화한다.
  - root cause: 출발점은 운영 소유 시스템의 범위와 공유 개념 기준의 범위를 동일시한 boundary-contract 판단이다. 연체/default 상태의 operational authority가 외부 시스템에 있다는 사실은 그 시스템이 상태값을 관리한다는 뜻이지, 공유 온톨로지가 DelinquencyStatus 또는 CreditImpairmentStatus 같은 reference concept을 전혀 갖지 않아도 된다는 뜻은 아니다.
  - causal path: 대상은 연체/부실 상태를 별도 시스템에서 다룬다는 이유로 온톨로지 범위 밖이라고 선언한다. 그 결과 entities와 relations에는 Borrower 또는 Exposure에 연결되는 연체·부실 status, 상태 기준시점, lifecycle transition, reporting bucket, source system 관계가 없다. 이 누락 때문에 공유 분류 기준은 정상 익스포저에서 부실화 이후 구간으로 넘어가는 상태 lifecycle을 표현하거나 보고·엔진 분류와 연결하지 못한다.
  - action: 외부 연체관리 시스템의 운영 권위는 유지하되, 공유 온톨로지에는 최소 reference entity로 DelinquencyStatus 또는 CreditImpairmentStatus를 포함해야 한다. 이 reference concept에는 status value, status_as_of, source_system, lifecycle transition, reporting_bucket, Borrower/Exposure relationship을 정의해 issue-005의 boundary-contract 수리와 함께 닫을 수 있도록 해야 한다.

- issue-009 (medium): `Collateral is_a Exposure` should be treated as a material semantic modeling error: collateral is a pledged asset securing an exposure, not a subtype of the credit exposure itself.
  - issue stance agreement: 3/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, semantics, structure
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: coverage, evolution
  - insufficient evidence lenses: logic
  - resolution accepted by: 3/3 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: semantics, structure, axiology
  - remaining disagreement: 0/3 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: semantics
  - issue statement: Collateral is_a Exposure 선언은 pledged asset을 credit exposure subtype처럼 만들며 Exposure secured_by Collateral relation과 의미가 충돌한다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml entities.Collateral Source finding context: materialized-input.md:39-46, materialized-input.md:87 Source finding context: Collateral is modeled as an Exposure even though its definition gives it the meaning of a pledged asset. Source finding context: The target itself distinguishes `Exposure` as the loan/credit position and `Collateral` as the securing asset, and the relation `Exposure -> Collateral secured_by` already expresses their association. Treating the securing asset as a subtype of the secured exposure misstates the ontological type and can make inherited exposure semantics apply to collateral records. Source finding context: Remove `Collateral.is_a: Exposure`; keep `Collateral` as a separate entity and express the link only through a relation such as `Exposure secured_by Collateral` or an explicit collateral reference/join entity if needed. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: The ontology is intended as a shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems. Source finding context: Any consumer that interprets `is_a` as inheritance or subtype membership will treat collateral assets as exposure instances. Source finding context: Risk and reporting consumers may apply exposure attributes, aggregation, or classification assumptions to collateral assets, weakening trust in the shared concept boundary between credit positions and pledged assets. Source finding context: The ontology uses subtype inheritance for a semantic association that is already represented as a securing relation. Source finding context: `Collateral` is declared with `is_a: Exposure`. Source finding context: `Collateral` is defined as an asset securing an exposure, while `Exposure` is defined as the credit exposure itself. Source finding context: The model also declares `Exposure secured_by Collateral`, showing the intended meaning is association rather than subtype identity.
  - affected purpose: The ontology is intended as a shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems.
  - failure condition: Any consumer that interprets is_a as inheritance or subtype membership will treat collateral assets as exposure instances. Source finding context: Any consumer that interprets `is_a` as inheritance or subtype membership will treat collateral assets as exposure instances.
  - impact: Risk and reporting consumers may apply exposure attributes, aggregation, or classification assumptions to collateral assets, weakening trust in the shared concept boundary between credit positions and pledged assets.
  - root hypothesis: Collateral에 subtype inheritance를 사용해, 이미 secured_by relation으로 표현된 association을 subtype identity로 잘못 모델링했다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001`, `materialized-input.md:24-37`, `materialized-input.md:39-46`, `materialized-input.md:85-88`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-013`, `finding-013.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:39-42`, `finding-013.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:24-25`, `materialized-input.md:39-40`, `finding-013.cause-003`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.coverage`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-009`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-009`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-009.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: The ontology is meant to be a shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems. If consumers interpret `is_a` as inheritance or subtype membership, collateral assets can be processed as exposure instances, causing exposure attributes, aggregation rules, or classifications to be applied to pledged assets and weakening the boundary between credit positions and their security.
  - root cause: The causal chain starts with using subtype inheritance to represent an association. `Collateral` is modeled under `Exposure` even though the intended association is already captured by `Exposure secured_by Collateral`, so the source of the defect is the mistaken subtype identity rather than a missing relation.
  - causal path: The evidence path shows that `Collateral` is declared with `is_a: Exposure`; its definition instead describes an asset securing an exposure; and the model separately declares `Exposure secured_by Collateral`. Together these show that collateral and exposure are distinct concepts linked by association, so the subtype declaration misstates the ontological type and creates inherited exposure semantics for collateral records.
  - action: Remove `Collateral.is_a: Exposure`, keep `Collateral` as a separate entity, and preserve the connection through `Exposure secured_by Collateral` or an explicit join/reference entity if the model needs richer collateral linkage. This should be fixed in the target now because the inherited subtype meaning is the mechanism that can mislead downstream consumers.

- issue-010 (medium): issue-010은 LTV가 파생 담보비율처럼 명명되고 배치되었지만 독립적인 수동 입력 권위를 가진다는 문제다. formula, collateral selection, valuation/date basis, rule/version authority, override policy, provenance가 없어서 계산된 LTV와 수동 입력 LTV의 의미와 권위가 닫히지 않는다.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: semantics, structure, axiology, coverage, evolution
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: logic
  - resolution accepted by: 5/5 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: semantics, axiology, coverage, evolution, structure
  - remaining disagreement: 0/5 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: none
  - raised by lenses: semantics
  - issue statement: Exposure.ltv는 collateral ratio라는 이름을 갖지만 Exposure.amount와 Collateral.appraised_value 옆에서 formula, valuation basis, timestamp, override semantics 없이 수동 입력된다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml Exposure.ltv Source finding context: materialized-input.md:29, materialized-input.md:35-37, materialized-input.md:45 Source finding context: `ltv` is named as a collateral ratio but modeled as an independent manually entered input beside its source quantities. Source finding context: A ratio/percentage concept is semantically a derived measure when its source quantities are present in the same concept model. Modeling it as an independent input without defining its formula, valuation basis, timestamp, or override semantics creates a derived-value/input mismatch: the same exposure can have an amount, collateral value, and LTV that do not agree. Source finding context: Define `ltv` as a derived measure with explicit formula, collateral selection rules, valuation date basis, and override policy; if manual underwriting overrides must exist, name them separately, for example `underwriter_ltv_override`, and preserve provenance. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: The ontology is meant to give risk engines and reports a shared meaning for exposure, limit, and collateral concepts. Source finding context: A consumer compares or recalculates LTV from `Exposure.amount` and `Collateral.appraised_value`, while another consumer trusts the manually entered `Exposure.ltv`. Source finding context: Risk classification and reporting can diverge on the same exposure because the ratio name implies a derived concept but the model gives it independent input authority. Source finding context: The ontology does not distinguish derived LTV from a manually asserted underwriting value. Source finding context: `Exposure.ltv` is a number with a note saying it is manually entered by an underwriter. Source finding context: The same ontology includes `Exposure.amount` and `Collateral.appraised_value`, which are source quantities for a collateral ratio. Source finding context: No formula, valuation basis, or override semantics are declared for reconciling the manually entered value with those source quantities.
  - affected purpose: The ontology is meant to give risk engines and reports a shared meaning for exposure, limit, and collateral concepts.
  - failure condition: A consumer compares or recalculates LTV from Exposure.amount and Collateral.appraised_value, while another consumer trusts the manually entered Exposure.ltv. Source finding context: A consumer compares or recalculates LTV from `Exposure.amount` and `Collateral.appraised_value`, while another consumer trusts the manually entered `Exposure.ltv`.
  - impact: Risk classification and reporting can diverge on the same exposure because the ratio name implies a derived concept but the model gives it independent input authority.
  - root hypothesis: LTV가 source quantities가 있는 derived ratio인지 manually asserted underwriting input인지 구분되지 않고 독립 수동 입력값으로 모델링되었다.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002`, `materialized-input.md:29`, `materialized-input.md:35-37`, `materialized-input.md:45`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-014`, `finding-014.cause-001`, `finding-014.cause-002`, `finding-014.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:39-46`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.coverage`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-010`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-010`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-010.structure`, `issue-ledger.yaml#dep-004`, `rel-010`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - materiality: 이 온톨로지는 risk engine과 report가 exposure, limit, collateral 개념을 같은 의미로 쓰게 하는 것이 목적이다. 그런데 한 소비자는 Exposure.amount와 Collateral.appraised_value로 LTV를 재계산하고, 다른 소비자는 수동 입력된 Exposure.ltv를 신뢰할 수 있다. 같은 exposure에 대해 위험 분류와 보고 값이 갈라질 수 있으므로 목적을 직접 약화한다.
  - root cause: 시작점은 LTV가 source quantities가 있는 derived collateral ratio인지, underwriter가 수동으로 주장한 값인지 구분되지 않는 것이다. 이 구분이 없기 때문에 Exposure.ltv가 독립 입력값처럼 권위를 가지면서도 ratio라는 이름과 주변 source quantity 배치가 계산값처럼 해석되게 만든다.
  - causal path: causal path는 Exposure.ltv가 underwriter 수동 입력 number로 존재한다는 점에서 시작한다. 같은 모델 안에 Exposure.amount와 Collateral.appraised_value라는 ratio 산출 source quantity가 함께 있으므로 LTV는 계산 가능한 derived measure로 해석될 수 있다. 그러나 formula, valuation basis, timestamp, override semantics가 선언되지 않아 수동 값과 계산값을 조정할 기준이 없고, 그 결과 derived-value/input mismatch가 발생한다.
  - action: ltv를 formula authority, collateral selection rule, valuation date basis, temporal basis, recalculation rule/version semantics를 가진 derived measure로 정의해야 한다. 수동 underwriting 값이 필요하면 underwriter_ltv_override 같은 별도 concept으로 분리하고 provenance, 승인/정당화, override policy를 붙여야 한다. 이 조치는 issue-006의 broader audit-event gap과 구분되는 LTV-specific authority 문제를 target 내에서 닫기 위한 fix-now 사항이다.

- issue-011 (medium): Issue-011 is a material, focused rating-scale mapping gap: Borrower.internal_rating, RiskRating.grade, and Exposure.risk_grade look like related credit/risk grade concepts, but the ontology does not define their shared authority, scope, conversions, or override semantics.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: coverage, evolution, semantics, structure, axiology
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: logic
  - resolution accepted by: 4/5 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: semantics, coverage, evolution, structure
  - remaining disagreement: 1/5 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: axiology
  - raised by lenses: semantics
  - issue statement: Borrower internal rating, RiskRating.grade, and Exposure.risk_grade are shared-looking rating concepts, but their mappings, scopes, manual adjustments, and conversion authority are not defined inside the ontology. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml rating concepts Source finding context: materialized-input.md:20-22, materialized-input.md:31-34, materialized-input.md:48-57, materialized-input.md:92-98 Source finding context: Three credit-rating scales are presented as shared concepts without an authoritative semantic mapping inside the ontology. Source finding context: These names all denote credit/risk grade semantics, but their scopes and transformations are not made authoritative in the shared ontology. A risk engine and a reporting system can therefore use the same ontology while assigning different meanings to equivalent grade levels or treating a manual reporting adjustment as if it were the engine-derived risk grade. Source finding context: Add an explicit canonical rating concept or mapping table in the ontology, including scope (`borrower` vs `exposure`), scale ownership, effective dates, derivation rules, and override/provenance semantics for report-side adjustments. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: The target says it is a concept authority shared by a risk engine and reporting system. Source finding context: The risk engine emits `RiskRating.grade`, reports consume or adjust `Exposure.risk_grade`, and consumers rely on external or local transformations rather than an ontology-owned mapping. Source finding context: The central risk-grade concept is not semantically stable across systems, so classifications and reports can disagree while both appear conformant to the ontology. Source finding context: The ontology delegates rating-scale equivalence and conversion semantics to external consumer-specific mappings. Source finding context: The ontology defines three rating-like fields with distinct scales: 1-10, R1-R5, and AAA-CCC. Source finding context: The classification rule says `Exposure.risk_grade` is derived from `RiskRating.grade` but can be manually adjusted by the reporting team. Source finding context: The note says the three scales coexist for historical reasons and conversion is performed by each consuming system.
  - affected purpose: The target says it is a concept authority shared by a risk engine and reporting system.
  - failure condition: The risk engine emits RiskRating.grade, reports consume or adjust Exposure.risk_grade, and consumers rely on external or local transformations rather than an ontology-owned mapping. Source finding context: The risk engine emits `RiskRating.grade`, reports consume or adjust `Exposure.risk_grade`, and consumers rely on external or local transformations rather than an ontology-owned mapping.
  - impact: The central risk-grade concept is not semantically stable across systems, so classifications and reports can disagree while both appear conformant to the ontology.
  - root hypothesis: Three credit-rating scales have no ontology-owned authoritative semantic mapping, leaving equivalence and conversion semantics to external consumer-specific mechanisms.
  - evidence: `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003`, `materialized-input.md:6-8`, `materialized-input.md:20-22`, `materialized-input.md:31-34`, `materialized-input.md:48-57`, `materialized-input.md:92-98`, `finding-ledger.yaml#finding-015`, `finding-015.cause-001`, `materialized-input.md:53-56`, `finding-015.cause-002`, `materialized-input.md:92-93`, `finding-015.cause-003`, `materialized-input.md:97-98`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.axiology`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.coverage`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/issue-ledger.yaml#issue-011`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.evolution`, `issue-ledger.yaml#issue-011`, `rel-001`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.logic`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.semantics`, `issue-stance-matrix.yaml#stances.issue-011.structure`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/issue-ledger.yaml`, `.onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/problem-framing.yaml`
  - source lenses: semantics
  - action candidates: follow_up
  - materiality: This weakens the target purpose because the ontology is meant to be a shared concept authority for a risk engine and reporting system. If the engine emits RiskRating.grade while reports consume or manually adjust Exposure.risk_grade through local transformations, both systems can appear conformant while assigning different meanings to the same risk-grade semantics.
  - root cause: The starting point is the absence of an ontology-owned authoritative semantic mapping across the three credit-rating scales. Without a canonical rating concept or mapping authority, equivalence, conversion, effective dating, and scale ownership are left to consumer-specific mechanisms.
  - causal path: The causal chain begins with three rating-like fields using distinct scales, then adds a rule that Exposure.risk_grade is derived from RiskRating.grade but can be manually adjusted by reporting, and culminates in a note that conversions are performed by each consuming system. That path supports the issue because the ontology exposes shared-looking rating concepts while leaving the meaning-changing transformation and adjustment rules outside its authority.
  - action: Add an explicit canonical rating concept or ontology-owned mapping table covering borrower versus exposure scope, scale ownership, effective dates, derivation rules, conversion authority, and override/provenance semantics. This should remain linked or subordinate to the broader governed-classification authority work in issue-001, but it needs rating-specific traceability so the risk engine and reports share the same grade semantics.
  - unresolved disagreement: Axiology narrows the issue as a subordinate expression of issue-001's broader governed classification authority problem, while semantics, coverage, evolution, and structure support carrying it forward as a separate focused rating-scale mapping issue.

#### Non-Material Findings
- none

#### Action Candidates
- issue-001: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: 다수 렌즈와 deliberation resolution이 broad governed classification authority 결함을 유지했다. Semantics는 rating/classification meaning authority 범위로 증거를 좁혔으므로 판단은 contested로 보존하되, 공유 권위 문서라는 목적을 직접 약화하는 root-level governance 문제다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml classification_rules / notes Source finding context: classification_rules[0]; notes[0]; header purpose comment Source finding context: 보고서 수동 조정과 소비자별 등급 변환은 공유 개념 권위라는 목적에서 drift를 만든다. Source finding context: 이 온톨로지는 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 문서를 지향한다. 그런데 등급의 도출·조정·변환 권위를 소비 시스템과 보고서 팀에 분산하면 같은 차주/익스포저가 엔진·보고서·소비 시스템별로 다른 리스크 의미를 갖게 된다. 이는 운영 유연성이라는 국소 최적화가 공유 기준이라는 핵심 목적을 약화하는 트레이드오프다. Source finding context: 등급 체계의 canonical source와 변환 테이블을 온톨로지 안에 명시하고, 수동 조정은 별도 override 개념으로 분리해 사유, 적용 범위, 승인 권위, 원등급 보존, 유효기간을 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 개념 권위 문서로 사용되는 것. Source finding context: RiskRating.grade, Exposure.risk_grade, Borrower.internal_rating 간 변환 또는 보고 조정이 시스템별로 다르게 적용될 때. Source finding context: 동일 리스크 판단이 시스템별로 달라져 보고 수치와 엔진 판단의 비교·추적·감사가 약해진다. Source finding context: 등급 의미와 변환/조정 권위가 온톨로지 내부의 단일 권위로 닫혀 있지 않다. Source finding context: 대상은 공유 개념 권위 문서를 지향한다고 선언한다. Source finding context: 하지만 등급 변환은 각 소비 시스템이 수행하도록 되어 있다. Source finding context: 보고서 팀은 엔진 산출 등급에서 도출되는 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정할 수 있다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml rating scale model Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:15-17, 26-29, 48-51, 87-93 Source finding context: Rating scale evolution is not centralized, so future grade changes require coordinated consumer rewrites and can break shared continuity. Source finding context: For an ontology intended to be the shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems, the extensible concept should be the rating scale plus versioned mapping rule. Here the mapping authority is outside the target and distributed to consumers. If the engine adds a grade, reporting changes buckets, or historical mappings are revised, continuity depends on every consumer updating its own conversion logic consistently rather than on the ontology preserving versioned scale/mapping semantics. Source finding context: Promote rating scale and mapping into first-class ontology concepts, with scale id, scale version/effective dates, source authority, mapping table, and manual-override provenance. Keep consumer systems reading the same canonical mapping rather than each implementing conversion independently. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: The ontology's declared purpose as a shared concept authority for the risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: A new or revised rating scale, mapping, or manual override policy is introduced after v1.1. Source finding context: Risk and reporting consumers can classify the same borrower or exposure differently, and historical grade continuity cannot be reconstructed from the ontology alone. Source finding context: The ontology models rating values as separate local attributes/enums while delegating conversion authority to external or per-consumer mechanisms. Source finding context: Three rating scales are represented separately in the target: borrower internal rating, engine rating, and exposure/reporting grade. Source finding context: The target says derivation and conversion are not fully governed inside the ontology: manual reporting adjustment is allowed, mappings are in a wiki, and each consumer performs conversion. Source finding context: Because conversion authority is distributed, adding or changing a rating category requires synchronized changes outside the ontology and weakens historical continuity. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml closed classification enums Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:14, 25, 39 Source finding context: Core classification axes are closed enums without extension/version hooks, making new segments, products, or collateral types a core schema change. Source finding context: Credit risk taxonomies usually evolve as new borrower segments, lending products, guarantees, or collateral classes appear. Under the current shape, any new value is invalid until the central ontology is edited and all downstream validators/report mappings are changed. That makes ordinary business expansion a schema migration rather than a controlled code-set extension. Source finding context: Replace or supplement fixed inline enums with governed code-set concepts: canonical code, display label, parent category, effective_from/effective_to, status, owner, and compatibility mapping. Reserve inline enum lists only for truly closed states. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002 Source finding context: The taxonomy's suitability as a shared classification basis across risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: A new borrower segment, product type, or collateral type must be onboarded while existing consumers still rely on v1.1 validation and reporting categories. Source finding context: New legitimate records can be rejected or inconsistently bucketed, and the ontology must be structurally revised for routine classification growth. Source finding context: The ontology encodes business classifications as closed inline enums instead of governed extensible code sets. Source finding context: Several domain classifications are represented as fixed enum arrays. Source finding context: No adjacent metadata defines extension ownership, lifecycle, versioning, aliases, or backward-compatible mappings for those enum values. Source finding context: A future valid category therefore requires editing the core ontology and coordinating downstream consumers rather than adding a governed classification member.

RiskRating, ExposureRiskGrade, LimitApproval/LimitVersion, RiskAppetiteVersion처럼 기준일 재구성이 필요한 개념을 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고서 시스템이 공유하는 여신 리스크 개념 권위 문서 Source finding context: 보고 시스템이 과거 기준일의 등급, 한도, LTV, 리스크 성향 준수상태를 재현하거나 엔진 산출값과 대사해야 하는 경우 Source finding context: 현재 온톨로지는 일부 집계와 담보 평가 시점만 보존하므로 보고 기준일 값의 근거를 같은 개념 기준으로 닫을 수 없어 엔진/보고 간 신뢰와 감사 가능성이 약해진다. Source finding context: 시간성 적용 범위가 일부 산출물과 평가일에만 국소적으로 존재하고, 등급·한도·상태 값 전반의 공통 이력 개념으로 모델링되지 않았다. Source finding context: 대상 온톨로지는 ExposureAggregate.as_of와 Collateral.appraised_at만 명시적 시점 속성을 제공한다. Source finding context: RiskRating.grade/score, Exposure.risk_grade/ltv, Limit.approved_amount, RiskAppetite.threshold/compliance_status에는 유효기간이나 이력 속성이 없다. Source finding context: 따라서 기준일 집계가 있어도 그 기준일에 적용된 등급·한도·성향 기준을 온톨로지 내부 개념만으로 재구성할 수 없다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml time-dependent derivation rules Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:64-71, 87-90 Source finding context: Time-sensitive calculations depend on batch timing and per-system tables without versioned calculation context, weakening continuity when rules or market data change. Source finding context: Evolution pressure here comes from changing FX sources, batch frequencies, reporting cutoffs, valuation policies, or revaluation thresholds. The ontology records some timestamps, but not the versioned calculation context needed to keep old outputs comparable after those operational rules change. Risk engine and reporting systems can therefore preserve the same field names while silently changing meaning over time. Source finding context: Add calculation-context concepts for aggregates and derived metrics: as-of timestamp, batch/run id, FX source and rate date, rule version, inclusion cutoff, and revaluation policy version. Make derived fields reference the context used to produce them. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003 Source finding context: Shared interpretation of risk calculations and reports over time. Source finding context: FX tables, batch cadence, cutoff policy, collateral revaluation threshold, or report timing changes after records have already been produced. Source finding context: Historical and current values with the same ontology fields may no longer be comparable, making risk trend reporting and engine/report reconciliation unreliable. Source finding context: The ontology stores or defines derived risk values without carrying the full versioned operational context that produced them. Source finding context: Exposure aggregates are stored from a month-end batch and explicitly omit intraday loans until the next batch. Source finding context: Utilization conversion is delegated to each system's daily FX table, and collateral revaluation is expressed as an elapsed-time rule without a policy version. Source finding context: Because the source table, cutoff, and rule version are not modeled, later operational changes can alter calculation meaning without a traceable ontology-level continuity mechanism. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / entities.ExposureAggregate Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:64-71 Source finding context: ExposureAggregate.total_amount is defined as both the sum of all Exposure.amount and a month-end batch value that excludes intraday new exposures until the next batch. Source finding context: In any state after a new intraday exposure exists and before the next batch, the declared definition requires inclusion of that Exposure.amount, but the attribute note permits non-inclusion. Both claims cannot be true for the same borrower, as_of date, and exposure set. This matters because the artifact is intended as a shared authority for risk engines and reporting systems; a utilization or appetite calculation using this concept can be simultaneously 'correct' and 'incorrect' depending on which claim is applied. Source finding context: Make the temporal boundary explicit. For example, define ExposureAggregate as the sum of Exposure.amount as of a specific batch cutoff/as_of timestamp, or split it into CurrentExposureAggregate and MonthEndExposureAggregate so the universal 'all Exposure.amount' claim is not attached to a lagged stored value. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems using exposure aggregates in risk and utilization calculations. Source finding context: A borrower receives an intraday new exposure after the month-end batch and before the next batch, while ExposureAggregate.total_amount is used as the authoritative total exposure. Source finding context: The same ontology permits two incompatible truth conditions for the aggregate, weakening review trust and making downstream calculations non-actionable without an extra unstated temporal convention. Source finding context: The aggregate concept lacks a single temporal authority: its definition uses an unqualified universal sum, while its attribute note authorizes a lagged batch projection. Source finding context: ExposureAggregate is defined as borrower-level total exposure and the sum of all Exposure.amount. Source finding context: ExposureAggregate.total_amount is stored by a month-end batch and intraday new exposures are not reflected until the next batch. Source finding context: Limit utilization is calculated from ExposureAggregate.total_amount, so the contradiction affects a declared downstream rule inside the target.

Source finding context: entities 목록에는 해당 행위의 행위자·시각·근거·변경 전후 값을 담는 이벤트나 증거 엔티티가 없다. Source finding context: 그 결과 조정·승인된 값은 공유될 수 있지만, 통제 행위의 정당성과 감사 추적은 공유 개념 범위 밖에 남는다.

Source finding context: SourceOfRecord 또는 AuthorityPolicy 개념을 추가하고 rating_scale_mapping, fx_rate_table, available_limit, approved_amount별 canonical_source, precedence, effective_date, fallback, reconciliation_rule을 명시한다. 외부 위키/CRM/시스템 테이블은 단순 참고가 아니라 권위와 버전을 가진 참조 대상으로 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-003 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고서 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 문서 Source finding context: 소비 시스템별 등급 변환, 환율 테이블, CRM 한도 값이 서로 달라 한도 소진율이나 보고 등급이 달라지는 경우 Source finding context: 공유 온톨로지가 우선순위와 단일 원본을 정하지 않아 동일 개념의 시스템별 해석 차이를 해소하지 못한다. Source finding context: 병행 관리되는 값과 외부 참조를 source-of-record/precedence 개념으로 승격하지 않고 주석 수준에 남겼다. Source finding context: 대상 텍스트는 등급 변환, CRM 한도, 환율 테이블이 여러 소비 시스템 또는 외부 장소에 흩어져 있음을 명시한다. Source finding context: 온톨로지에는 SourceOfRecord, MappingAuthority, FXRateAuthority, precedence 같은 권위 지정 엔티티나 속성이 없다. Source finding context: 따라서 시스템 간 값 충돌이 발생해도 공유 개념 기준이 어느 값을 채택할지 결정하지 못한다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-003: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: Semantics와 structure가 직접 지지하고 다른 렌즈도 평가 결과와 dependency 개념의 결여를 인정한다. 공유 graph contract로 breach와 utilization을 소비하게 하려면 policy와 evaluated result 및 dependency edge의 의미 일관성이 target 안에서 닫혀야 한다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml RiskAppetite Source finding context: materialized-input.md:78-83, materialized-input.md:94 Source finding context: `RiskAppetite` conflates appetite policy, numeric threshold, and compliance result in one concept. Source finding context: Risk appetite is a policy or constraint, while compliance status is an assessment result at a point in time. Combining them blurs whether `RiskAppetite` means the policy, its limit value, or the current evaluated state, and the classification rule for limit utilization separately depends on exposure aggregate and approved amount. Source finding context: Separate the policy/limit concept from the evaluation result: for example, keep `RiskAppetite` with threshold, currency/scope/effective period, and create `RiskAppetiteCompliance` with borrower or portfolio scope, aggregate reference, evaluated value, status, and as-of time. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-004 Source finding context: The ontology is intended to coordinate risk-engine decisions and reporting concepts for limits and appetite breaches. Source finding context: A consumer treats `RiskAppetite.compliance_status` as an intrinsic property of the appetite policy rather than a time-scoped result of evaluating exposure against a threshold. Source finding context: Reports may present stale or scope-ambiguous breach states as policy facts, and engines may lack a stable semantic distinction between limit definition and compliance evaluation. Source finding context: The concept boundary between a risk appetite constraint and its evaluated compliance state is not separated. Source finding context: `RiskAppetite` is defined as a limit and also contains `compliance_status`. Source finding context: The definition compares `ExposureAggregate` to `RiskAppetite` directly, although the comparable numeric attribute is `threshold`. Source finding context: `ExposureAggregate` is explicitly time-scoped with `as_of`, but `RiskAppetite.compliance_status` has no corresponding evaluation time or scope. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml RiskAppetite entity and relations graph Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:73-85 Source finding context: RiskAppetite is structurally disconnected from the aggregate it governs. Source finding context: The definition says ExposureAggregate crossing RiskAppetite determines a violation, while the only declared relations connect Borrower, Exposure, Collateral, RiskRating, Limit, and ExposureAggregate. Without a relation, shared consumers cannot traverse from an aggregate to the applicable appetite threshold or compliance status. Source finding context: Add an explicit relation such as `{ from: ExposureAggregate, to: RiskAppetite, kind: measured_against }` or `{ from: RiskAppetite, to: ExposureAggregate, kind: applies_to }`, plus any key/scope attributes needed to select the applicable appetite. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-001 Source finding context: The ontology is intended as the shared concept authority for a risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: When a consumer needs to determine or report RiskAppetite compliance from ExposureAggregate, the target has no declared structural path connecting the two concepts. Source finding context: This weakens actionability and review trust because a central violation concept is present but not reachable through the ontology's relation graph. Source finding context: RiskAppetite was added as an entity with a definition-level dependency on ExposureAggregate but without a corresponding relation edge in the ontology graph. Source finding context: RiskAppetite defines compliance by comparing ExposureAggregate to RiskAppetite. Source finding context: The relations list does not include RiskAppetite in any from/to position. Source finding context: Therefore the compliance concept is disconnected from the aggregate it depends on. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml Limit utilization rule and relations graph Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:54-71,80-89 Source finding context: The Limit utilization rule depends on ExposureAggregate and Limit, but the relation graph does not connect those concepts. Source finding context: A consumer following the ontology graph can see that both concepts refer to Borrower, but cannot identify the intended structural dependency for utilization or whether the ratio is borrower-level, product-level, or another scoped join. This is a missing relation for a declared rule, not merely a semantic preference. Source finding context: Declare the utilization dependency explicitly, for example `{ from: ExposureAggregate, to: Limit, kind: consumes }` or a named derived concept/relationship for `limit_utilization`, with scope keys that align aggregate and limit selection. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/structure.findings.yaml#structure-candidate-002 Source finding context: The ontology is intended to give risk and reporting systems a shared structural basis for classification and rule consumption. Source finding context: When implementing or auditing the declared Limit utilization rule, consumers must infer the ExposureAggregate-to-Limit join path from parallel Borrower references rather than from an explicit relation. Source finding context: The missing structural edge makes a stated rule under-specified as a graph contract and can lead consumers to implement divergent joins while still appearing to follow the ontology. Source finding context: A classification rule introduced a cross-entity dependency without adding the matching relation to the ontology graph. Source finding context: Limit and ExposureAggregate are separate entities with borrower_ref attributes. Source finding context: The declared utilization formula combines ExposureAggregate.total_amount with Limit.approved_amount. Source finding context: The relations list connects each concept to Borrower but does not connect ExposureAggregate to Limit or define a utilization relation.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-004: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: 비-logic 렌즈들이 FX calculation authority gap을 지지하지만 logic contradiction은 독립적으로 제시되지 않았다. 동일 포지션의 utilization이 시스템별 FX table에 따라 달라질 수 있으므로 다음 단계 전에 canonical calculation authority로 닫아야 한다. Source finding context: classification_rules[1] currency conversion Source finding context: classification_rules[1]; Limit.attributes.currency Source finding context: 통화 환산 권위를 각 시스템에 맡겨 한도 소진율의 공유 기준을 약화한다. Source finding context: 한도 소진율은 공유 리스크/보고 기준이어야 하는데 환율 출처가 시스템별이면 동일 익스포저와 한도에서도 다른 소진율이 산출될 수 있다. 이는 시스템 자율성을 위해 canonical 계산 권위를 포기하는 숨은 트레이드오프다. Source finding context: 환율 기준일, 환율 소스, 우선순위, 재계산 정책을 온톨로지의 canonical 계산 규칙으로 정하거나 ExchangeRateSource 같은 권위 개념을 추가한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-003 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 한도 소진율 계산 기준. Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템의 당일 환율 테이블 값이나 갱신 시각이 다를 때. Source finding context: 동일 포지션의 한도 소진율이 시스템별로 달라져 보고·통제 간 reconciliation 비용과 판단 불일치를 만든다. Source finding context: 환율 변환의 계산 권위가 온톨로지에 닫혀 있지 않고 소비 시스템에 분산되어 있다. Source finding context: Limit은 통화를 속성으로 갖는다. Source finding context: 통화가 다른 경우 환산은 각 시스템의 환율 테이블을 사용한다. Source finding context: 따라서 한도 소진율 계산 결과가 시스템별 환율 상태에 의존한다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-005: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - rationale: 연체/부실은 핵심 credit-risk state이나 외부 authority, identifier, as-of time, reporting relationship이 없다. 운영 소유권은 외부에 둘 수 있지만 공유 개념 기준의 boundary contract는 다음 단계 전에 필요하다. Source finding context: notes[1] default status boundary Source finding context: notes[1]; ontology header purpose Source finding context: 연체/부실 상태를 경계 밖으로만 둔 것은 여신 리스크 공유 기준의 목적상 중요한 경계 약속을 불충분하게 만든다. Source finding context: default 상태는 여신 리스크 분류와 보고에서 목적상 핵심적인 상태다. 별도 시스템 소관일 수는 있지만, 공유 개념 권위 문서라면 최소한 외부 권위, 참조 키, 반영 시점, 등급/보고와의 관계를 경계 계약으로 닫아야 한다. 단순 제외는 시스템 경계 편의가 리스크 의미의 완결성을 약화하는 선택이다. Source finding context: DefaultStatus를 외부 권위 개념으로 참조하거나, out-of-scope라도 external authority, 식별자, 갱신 시점, RiskRating/Exposure 보고 반영 규칙을 명시한 boundary contract를 추가한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-004 Source finding context: 여신 리스크 관리 개념 모델이 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템의 공유 기준이 되는 것. Source finding context: 차주나 익스포저가 연체/부실 상태인데 이 온톨로지의 등급·익스포저·보고 산출물과 연결해 해석해야 할 때. Source finding context: 핵심 리스크 상태가 공유 모델에서 끊겨 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템의 의미 정렬과 경계 책임 추적이 어려워진다. Source finding context: 목적상 핵심 외부 상태에 대한 boundary contract가 없다. Source finding context: 온톨로지는 여신 리스크 관리의 개념 모델과 공유 권위 문서를 지향한다. Source finding context: 연체/부실(default) 상태는 별도 시스템에서 다룬다는 이유로 범위 밖이라고만 정의된다. Source finding context: 외부 상태와 내부 리스크 등급/익스포저/보고 개념의 연결 규칙이 대상 안에 없다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-008: follow_up
  - rationale: deliberation resolution은 issue-008을 독립 root가 아니라 issue-005에 연결된 lifecycle/status facet으로 좁혔다. traceability를 위해 별도 분류하되, closure는 issue-005의 boundary-contract 수리와 함께 다음 단계에서 수행될 수 있다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / delinquency and default status coverage Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: notes state delinquency/default status is handled by a separate delinquency-management system and is out of this ontology's scope Source finding context: 연체·부실 상태 영역이 공유 리스크 분류 온톨로지에서 완전히 제외되어 상태 lifecycle 범위가 끊긴다. Source finding context: 대상 자체가 여신 리스크 관리의 개념 모델이자 엔진/보고 공유 기준을 지향한다고 선언한다. 그런데 파일 안에서 명시적으로 언급된 연체/부실 상태를 외부 시스템으로만 돌리면, 리스크 보고가 가장 민감하게 다루는 상태 전환을 같은 분류 권위 안에서 참조할 수 없다. Source finding context: 외부 시스템이 운영 권위를 유지하더라도 DelinquencyStatus 또는 CreditImpairmentStatus 같은 참조 엔티티를 온톨로지에 포함한다. 상태값, status_as_of, source_system, lifecycle transition, reporting_bucket, relationship to Exposure/Borrower를 최소 범위로 정의해 공유 분류 기준에는 포함되게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-004 Source finding context: 여신 리스크 관리 개념 모델과 리스크 엔진/보고 시스템의 공유 분류 기준 Source finding context: 보고서나 엔진이 익스포저의 연체·부실 여부를 등급, 한도, 리스크 성향 위반과 함께 분류·집계해야 하는 경우 Source finding context: 연체/부실 상태가 별도 시스템에 있다는 사실만으로는 공유 온톨로지에서 상태 의미와 lifecycle을 참조할 수 없어 핵심 리스크 상태 범위가 단절된다. Source finding context: 운영 소유 시스템의 범위와 공유 개념 기준의 범위를 동일시해 외부 소유 상태를 참조 개념으로도 포함하지 않았다. Source finding context: 대상은 연체/부실 상태를 별도 시스템에서 다룬다는 이유로 온톨로지 범위 밖이라고 선언한다. Source finding context: entities와 relations에는 Borrower 또는 Exposure의 연체·부실 상태나 상태 전환 개념이 없다. Source finding context: 따라서 공유 리스크 분류 기준은 익스포저 lifecycle의 부실화 이후 구간을 표현하지 못한다.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-009: fix_now
  - rationale: semantics와 structure가 직접 지지하는 type-modeling error이며 coverage/evolution은 lens scope 밖으로 보았을 뿐 반박하지 않았다. consumer가 is_a를 상속으로 해석하면 collateral asset에 exposure semantics가 적용되므로 target 안에서 바로 수정해야 하는 correctness 문제다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml entities.Collateral Source finding context: materialized-input.md:39-46, materialized-input.md:87 Source finding context: Collateral is modeled as an Exposure even though its definition gives it the meaning of a pledged asset. Source finding context: The target itself distinguishes `Exposure` as the loan/credit position and `Collateral` as the securing asset, and the relation `Exposure -> Collateral secured_by` already expresses their association. Treating the securing asset as a subtype of the secured exposure misstates the ontological type and can make inherited exposure semantics apply to collateral records. Source finding context: Remove `Collateral.is_a: Exposure`; keep `Collateral` as a separate entity and express the link only through a relation such as `Exposure secured_by Collateral` or an explicit collateral reference/join entity if needed. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-001 Source finding context: The ontology is intended as a shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems. Source finding context: Any consumer that interprets `is_a` as inheritance or subtype membership will treat collateral assets as exposure instances. Source finding context: Risk and reporting consumers may apply exposure attributes, aggregation, or classification assumptions to collateral assets, weakening trust in the shared concept boundary between credit positions and pledged assets. Source finding context: The ontology uses subtype inheritance for a semantic association that is already represented as a securing relation. Source finding context: `Collateral` is declared with `is_a: Exposure`. Source finding context: `Collateral` is defined as an asset securing an exposure, while `Exposure` is defined as the credit exposure itself. Source finding context: The model also declares `Exposure secured_by Collateral`, showing the intended meaning is association rather than subtype identity.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-010: fix_before_release, fix_now
  - rationale: deliberation은 derived-ratio/manual-input mismatch를 trigger로 유지하면서 LTV-specific derivation, valuation, override authority 문제로 좁혔다. 같은 exposure의 LTV 계산과 수동 입력 해석이 갈라질 수 있으므로 target 내에서 의미와 권위를 닫아야 한다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml Exposure.ltv Source finding context: materialized-input.md:29, materialized-input.md:35-37, materialized-input.md:45 Source finding context: `ltv` is named as a collateral ratio but modeled as an independent manually entered input beside its source quantities. Source finding context: A ratio/percentage concept is semantically a derived measure when its source quantities are present in the same concept model. Modeling it as an independent input without defining its formula, valuation basis, timestamp, or override semantics creates a derived-value/input mismatch: the same exposure can have an amount, collateral value, and LTV that do not agree. Source finding context: Define `ltv` as a derived measure with explicit formula, collateral selection rules, valuation date basis, and override policy; if manual underwriting overrides must exist, name them separately, for example `underwriter_ltv_override`, and preserve provenance. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-002 Source finding context: The ontology is meant to give risk engines and reports a shared meaning for exposure, limit, and collateral concepts. Source finding context: A consumer compares or recalculates LTV from `Exposure.amount` and `Collateral.appraised_value`, while another consumer trusts the manually entered `Exposure.ltv`. Source finding context: Risk classification and reporting can diverge on the same exposure because the ratio name implies a derived concept but the model gives it independent input authority. Source finding context: The ontology does not distinguish derived LTV from a manually asserted underwriting value. Source finding context: `Exposure.ltv` is a number with a note saying it is manually entered by an underwriter. Source finding context: The same ontology includes `Exposure.amount` and `Collateral.appraised_value`, which are source quantities for a collateral ratio. Source finding context: No formula, valuation basis, or override semantics are declared for reconciling the manually entered value with those source quantities.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`
- issue-011: follow_up
  - rationale: resolution은 issue-011을 issue-001에 종속·연결된 focused rating-scale mapping issue로 유지했다. Axiology는 broad classification authority issue의 표현으로 좁혔으므로 contested를 보존한다. rating-specific traceability 때문에 별도 carry-forward가 필요하지만 broader issue-001 remediation 중 함께 닫을 수 있다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml rating concepts Source finding context: materialized-input.md:20-22, materialized-input.md:31-34, materialized-input.md:48-57, materialized-input.md:92-98 Source finding context: Three credit-rating scales are presented as shared concepts without an authoritative semantic mapping inside the ontology. Source finding context: These names all denote credit/risk grade semantics, but their scopes and transformations are not made authoritative in the shared ontology. A risk engine and a reporting system can therefore use the same ontology while assigning different meanings to equivalent grade levels or treating a manual reporting adjustment as if it were the engine-derived risk grade. Source finding context: Add an explicit canonical rating concept or mapping table in the ontology, including scope (`borrower` vs `exposure`), scale ownership, effective dates, derivation rules, and override/provenance semantics for report-side adjustments. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/semantics.findings.yaml#semantics-candidate-003 Source finding context: The target says it is a concept authority shared by a risk engine and reporting system. Source finding context: The risk engine emits `RiskRating.grade`, reports consume or adjust `Exposure.risk_grade`, and consumers rely on external or local transformations rather than an ontology-owned mapping. Source finding context: The central risk-grade concept is not semantically stable across systems, so classifications and reports can disagree while both appear conformant to the ontology. Source finding context: The ontology delegates rating-scale equivalence and conversion semantics to external consumer-specific mappings. Source finding context: The ontology defines three rating-like fields with distinct scales: 1-10, R1-R5, and AAA-CCC. Source finding context: The classification rule says `Exposure.risk_grade` is derived from `RiskRating.grade` but can be manually adjusted by the reporting team. Source finding context: The note says the three scales coexist for historical reasons and conversion is performed by each consuming system.
  - derivation refs: `issue-ledger.yaml`, `problem-framing.yaml`

### Consensus (6/6, core-axis mode)
- issue-001 (high): issue-001은 등급 scale/mapping과 borrower/product/collateral 핵심 분류 코드가 온톨로지 소유의 버전 있는 공유 분류 권위로 닫혀 있지 않은 문제다. 이 때문에 등급 변환, 보고 조정, 등급 체계 변경, 일반 분류 확장이 소비자별 로직·수동 조정·폐쇄 enum에 분산된다.
  - issue stance agreement: 5/6
  - agreed or narrowed lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, structure, semantics
  - issue stance disagreement: 0/6
  - disagreeing stance lenses: none
  - not applicable lenses: none
  - insufficient evidence lenses: logic
  - resolution accepted by: 4/5 deliberation participants
  - accepted lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, structure
  - remaining disagreement: 1/5 deliberation participants
  - remaining disagreement lenses: semantics
  - raised by lenses: axiology, evolution
  - issue statement: 등급 변환, 보고 조정, 등급 스케일 진화, borrower/product/collateral 분류 확장이 모두 온톨로지 소유의 버전 있는 classification authority로 닫혀 있지 않다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml classification_rules / notes Source finding context: classification_rules[0]; notes[0]; header purpose comment Source finding context: 보고서 수동 조정과 소비자별 등급 변환은 공유 개념 권위라는 목적에서 drift를 만든다. Source finding context: 이 온톨로지는 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 문서를 지향한다. 그런데 등급의 도출·조정·변환 권위를 소비 시스템과 보고서 팀에 분산하면 같은 차주/익스포저가 엔진·보고서·소비 시스템별로 다른 리스크 의미를 갖게 된다. 이는 운영 유연성이라는 국소 최적화가 공유 기준이라는 핵심 목적을 약화하는 트레이드오프다. Source finding context: 등급 체계의 canonical source와 변환 테이블을 온톨로지 안에 명시하고, 수동 조정은 별도 override 개념으로 분리해 사유, 적용 범위, 승인 권위, 원등급 보존, 유효기간을 모델링한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-001 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 개념 권위 문서로 사용되는 것. Source finding context: RiskRating.grade, Exposure.risk_grade, Borrower.internal_rating 간 변환 또는 보고 조정이 시스템별로 다르게 적용될 때. Source finding context: 동일 리스크 판단이 시스템별로 달라져 보고 수치와 엔진 판단의 비교·추적·감사가 약해진다. Source finding context: 등급 의미와 변환/조정 권위가 온톨로지 내부의 단일 권위로 닫혀 있지 않다. Source finding context: 대상은 공유 개념 권위 문서를 지향한다고 선언한다. Source finding context: 하지만 등급 변환은 각 소비 시스템이 수행하도록 되어 있다. Source finding context: 보고서 팀은 엔진 산출 등급에서 도출되는 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정할 수 있다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml rating scale model Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:15-17, 26-29, 48-51, 87-93 Source finding context: Rating scale evolution is not centralized, so future grade changes require coordinated consumer rewrites and can break shared continuity. Source finding context: For an ontology intended to be the shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems, the extensible concept should be the rating scale plus versioned mapping rule. Here the mapping authority is outside the target and distributed to consumers. If the engine adds a grade, reporting changes buckets, or historical mappings are revised, continuity depends on every consumer updating its own conversion logic consistently rather than on the ontology preserving versioned scale/mapping semantics. Source finding context: Promote rating scale and mapping into first-class ontology concepts, with scale id, scale version/effective dates, source authority, mapping table, and manual-override provenance. Keep consumer systems reading the same canonical mapping rather than each implementing conversion independently. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-001 Source finding context: The ontology's declared purpose as a shared concept authority for the risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: A new or revised rating scale, mapping, or manual override policy is introduced after v1.1. Source finding context: Risk and reporting consumers can classify the same borrower or exposure differently, and historical grade continuity cannot be reconstructed from the ontology alone. Source finding context: The ontology models rating values as separate local attributes/enums while delegating conversion authority to external or per-consumer mechanisms. Source finding context: Three rating scales are represented separately in the target: borrower internal rating, engine rating, and exposure/reporting grade. Source finding context: The target says derivation and conversion are not fully governed inside the ontology: manual reporting adjustment is allowed, mappings are in a wiki, and each consumer performs conversion. Source finding context: Because conversion authority is distributed, adding or changing a rating category requires synchronized changes outside the ontology and weakens historical continuity. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml closed classification enums Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:14, 25, 39 Source finding context: Core classification axes are closed enums without extension/version hooks, making new segments, products, or collateral types a core schema change. Source finding context: Credit risk taxonomies usually evolve as new borrower segments, lending products, guarantees, or collateral classes appear. Under the current shape, any new value is invalid until the central ontology is edited and all downstream validators/report mappings are changed. That makes ordinary business expansion a schema migration rather than a controlled code-set extension. Source finding context: Replace or supplement fixed inline enums with governed code-set concepts: canonical code, display label, parent category, effective_from/effective_to, status, owner, and compatibility mapping. Reserve inline enum lists only for truly closed states. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-002 Source finding context: The taxonomy's suitability as a shared classification basis across risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: A new borrower segment, product type, or collateral type must be onboarded while existing consumers still rely on v1.1 validation and reporting categories. Source finding context: New legitimate records can be rejected or inconsistently bucketed, and the ontology must be structurally revised for routine classification growth. Source finding context: The ontology encodes business classifications as closed inline enums instead of governed extensible code sets. Source finding context: Several domain classifications are represented as fixed enum arrays. Source finding context: No adjacent metadata defines extension ownership, lifecycle, versioning, aliases, or backward-compatible mappings for those enum values. Source finding context: A future valid category therefore requires editing the core ontology and coordinating downstream consumers rather than adding a governed classification member.
  - affected purpose: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 개념 권위 문서 및 공유 분류 기준으로 사용되는 것. Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유할 개념 권위 문서로 사용되는 것. Source finding context: The ontology's declared purpose as a shared concept authority for the risk engine and reporting systems. Source finding context: The taxonomy's suitability as a shared classification basis across risk engine and reporting systems.
  - failure condition: RiskRating.grade, Exposure.risk_grade, Borrower.internal_rating 변환이나 보고 조정이 시스템별로 다르게 적용되거나, 새 등급·borrower segment·product type·collateral type이 도입될 때. Source finding context: RiskRating.grade, Exposure.risk_grade, Borrower.internal_rating 간 변환 또는 보고 조정이 시스템별로 다르게 적용될 때. Source finding context: A new or revised rating scale, mapping, or manual override policy is introduced after v1.1. Source finding context: A new borrower segment, product type, or collateral type must be onboarded while existing consumers still rely on v1.1 validation and reporting categories.
  - impact: 같은 borrower/exposure가 시스템별로 다르게 분류될 수 있고, 역사적 등급 연속성과 routine classification growth가 온톨로지 내부 기준만으로 재현·검증되지 않는다. Source finding context: 동일 리스크 판단이 시스템별로 달라져 보고 수치와 엔진 판단의 비교·추적·감사가 약해진다. Source finding context: Risk and reporting consumers can classify the same borrower or exposure differently, and historical grade continuity cannot be reconstructed from the ontology alone. Source finding context: New legitimate records can be rejected or inconsistently bucketed, and the ontology must be structurally revised for routine classification growth.
  - root hypothesis: 등급 값과 분류 코드가 버전 있는 canonical scale/code-set 개념이 아니라 로컬 속성·폐쇄 enum·외부/소비자별 매핑으로 남아 있어 등급 변경과 일반 분류 확장이 공유 권위 안에서 통제되지 않는다.

  - source lenses: axiology, evolution
  - action candidates: fix_before_release, follow_up
  - materiality: 온톨로지의 선언 목적은 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 및 분류 기준이 되는 것이다. 그런데 RiskRating.grade, Exposure.risk_grade, Borrower.internal_rating의 변환과 보고 조정이 시스템별 또는 보고팀 판단으로 분산되고, 주요 business classification이 폐쇄 enum으로 남아 있으면 같은 borrower/exposure가 엔진·보고·소비 시스템에서 다른 리스크 의미로 분류될 수 있다. 그 결과 보고 수치와 엔진 판단의 비교, 추적, 감사, 역사적 등급 연속성, 신규 분류 온보딩이 온톨로지 내부 기준만으로 재현·검증되지 않는다.
  - root cause: 출발점은 등급 값과 분류 코드가 canonical scale/code-set 개념으로 승격되지 않았다는 점이다. 이 값들이 owner, version/effective dates, lifecycle/status, compatibility mapping, override provenance를 가진 공유 권위가 아니라 로컬 속성, 폐쇄 enum, wiki 또는 소비자별 매핑으로 남아 있기 때문에 변경과 확장 권위가 온톨로지 밖으로 흩어진다.
  - causal path: finding-001은 공유 개념 권위라는 목적에도 불구하고 등급 변환은 각 소비 시스템이 수행하고 보고팀이 Exposure.risk_grade를 수동 조정할 수 있음을 보여준다. finding-009는 borrower internal rating, engine rating, exposure/reporting grade가 별도 rating surface로 존재하면서 매핑 권위가 온톨로지 내부에 버전 있게 중앙화되지 않아 미래 등급 변경 시 소비자별 동기화에 의존하게 됨을 설명한다. finding-010은 borrower segment, product type, collateral type 같은 핵심 분류축이 확장/version hook 없는 폐쇄 enum이어서 routine classification growth가 governed code-set 추가가 아니라 core schema 변경과 downstream 조정으로 번지는 경로를 보강한다.
  - action: 등급 scale과 mapping, 그리고 주요 borrower/product/collateral classification을 first-class governed concept으로 승격해야 한다. 각 개념에는 canonical code 또는 grade, owner/source authority, version 및 effective dates, lifecycle/status, compatibility mapping, alias/backward mapping, manual override provenance와 승인·범위·유효기간을 포함해야 한다. 이 조치는 다음 단계 전에 닫아야 하는 root-level governance 문제이며, 소비 시스템이 자체 변환을 구현하기보다 동일한 canonical mapping과 code-set authority를 읽도록 순서를 맞추는 것이 필요하다.
  - unresolved disagreement: Deliberation은 broad governed classification authority 이슈를 유지했다. Axiology, coverage, evolution, structure는 broad root를 수용했지만, semantics는 rating/classification meaning authority에는 동의하면서 closed-code-set evolution 전체 범위는 직접 증거 밖이라고 좁혔다. 이는 이슈를 축소할 실질 반박이 아니라 lens-scoped evidence limitation으로 남는다.

- issue-002 (high): 월말 배치 ExposureAggregate와 시간 의존 등급·한도·LTV·RiskAppetite 준수상태, 파생 계산이 공통 as_of, batch/run, cutoff, source, rule-version context 없이 같은 권위 값처럼 쓰이고 있어, 현재 온톨로지는 한도 소진율과 과거 리스크 상태를 신뢰성 있게 해석하거나 재현할 수 없다.

Source finding context: ExposureAggregate.total_amount and Limit utilization rule Source finding context: ExposureAggregate.attributes.total_amount.note; classification_rules[1] Source finding context: 월말 배치 집계가 한도 소진율의 기준값이 되면서 리스크 통제 목적보다 보고 편의가 우선된다. Source finding context: 한도 소진율은 여신 한도 관리의 운영 판단에 쓰이는 기준인데, 일중 신규 여신을 제외한 값을 공식 집계로 쓰면 한도 초과나 리스크 성향 위반을 늦게 인지할 수 있다. 시간성 비용이 명시적 통제 없이 수용되어 공유 리스크 기준의 목적을 훼손한다. Source finding context: ExposureAggregate를 reporting_snapshot과 control_current_exposure처럼 목적별로 분리하거나, total_amount의 freshness SLA, 포함/제외 범위, 일중 보정 방식, 한도 소진율에 사용할 권위 값을 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/axiology.findings.yaml#axiology-candidate-002 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 리스크/한도 판단 기준 제공. Source finding context: 일중 신규 여신이 발생했지만 월말 배치 전 한도 소진율이나 RiskAppetite 준수 상태가 계산될 때. Source finding context: 실제 익스포저보다 낮은 집계가 공식 계산에 사용되어 한도 관리와 보고 신뢰를 동시에 약화한다. Source finding context: 집계 값의 목적과 freshness 권위가 분리되지 않았다. Source finding context: ExposureAggregate.total_amount는 모든 Exposure.amount의 합으로 정의된다. Source finding context: 동일 필드는 월말 배치로 계산되어 일중 신규 여신을 다음 배치까지 반영하지 않는다. Source finding context: 그 값이 Limit 소진율 계산에 사용된다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / time-dependent risk and limit values Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: Borrower.internal_rating; Exposure.risk_grade and ltv; RiskRating.grade and score; Limit.approved_amount; RiskAppetite.threshold and compliance_status; only ExposureAggregate.as_of and Collateral.appraised_at carry explicit dates Source finding context: 시점 의존 값 대부분에 유효기간·이력 개념이 없어 과거 기준 리스크 상태를 재구성할 수 없다. Source finding context: 문서 목적은 리스크 엔진과 보고서 시스템이 공유하는 개념 권위 문서인데, 보고서는 특정 기준일의 등급·한도·담보인정비율·준수상태를 재현해야 한다. 대상 파일은 ExposureAggregate.total_amount에는 as_of를 두면서도 RiskRating, Exposure.risk_grade, Limit, RiskAppetite에는 동등한 시간 축을 두지 않아 기준일 보고값과 엔진 산출값의 재현 범위가 불균형하다. Source finding context: 시점 의존 엔티티나 속성을 값 객체/이력 엔티티로 분리하고, 최소한 valid_from, valid_to 또는 as_of, source_event_ref, version/status를 추가한다. RiskRating, ExposureRiskGrade, LimitApproval/LimitVersion, RiskAppetiteVersion처럼 기준일 재구성이 필요한 개념을 명시한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-001 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고서 시스템이 공유하는 여신 리스크 개념 권위 문서 Source finding context: 보고 시스템이 과거 기준일의 등급, 한도, LTV, 리스크 성향 준수상태를 재현하거나 엔진 산출값과 대사해야 하는 경우 Source finding context: 현재 온톨로지는 일부 집계와 담보 평가 시점만 보존하므로 보고 기준일 값의 근거를 같은 개념 기준으로 닫을 수 없어 엔진/보고 간 신뢰와 감사 가능성이 약해진다. Source finding context: 시간성 적용 범위가 일부 산출물과 평가일에만 국소적으로 존재하고, 등급·한도·상태 값 전반의 공통 이력 개념으로 모델링되지 않았다. Source finding context: 대상 온톨로지는 ExposureAggregate.as_of와 Collateral.appraised_at만 명시적 시점 속성을 제공한다. Source finding context: RiskRating.grade/score, Exposure.risk_grade/ltv, Limit.approved_amount, RiskAppetite.threshold/compliance_status에는 유효기간이나 이력 속성이 없다. Source finding context: 따라서 기준일 집계가 있어도 그 기준일에 적용된 등급·한도·성향 기준을 온톨로지 내부 개념만으로 재구성할 수 없다. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml time-dependent derivation rules Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:64-71, 87-90 Source finding context: Time-sensitive calculations depend on batch timing and per-system tables without versioned calculation context, weakening continuity when rules or market data change. Source finding context: Evolution pressure here comes from changing FX sources, batch frequencies, reporting cutoffs, valuation policies, or revaluation thresholds. The ontology records some timestamps, but not the versioned calculation context needed to keep old outputs comparable after those operational rules change. Risk engine and reporting systems can therefore preserve the same field names while silently changing meaning over time. Source finding context: Add calculation-context concepts for aggregates and derived metrics: as-of timestamp, batch/run id, FX source and rate date, rule version, inclusion cutoff, and revaluation policy version. Make derived fields reference the context used to produce them. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/evolution.findings.yaml#evolution-candidate-003 Source finding context: Shared interpretation of risk calculations and reports over time. Source finding context: FX tables, batch cadence, cutoff policy, collateral revaluation threshold, or report timing changes after records have already been produced. Source finding context: Historical and current values with the same ontology fields may no longer be comparable, making risk trend reporting and engine/report reconciliation unreliable. Source finding context: The ontology stores or defines derived risk values without carrying the full versioned operational context that produced them. Source finding context: Exposure aggregates are stored from a month-end batch and explicitly omit intraday loans until the next batch. Source finding context: Utilization conversion is delegated to each system's daily FX table, and collateral revaluation is expressed as an elapsed-time rule without a policy version. Source finding context: Because the source table, cutoff, and rule version are not modeled, later operational changes can alter calculation meaning without a traceable ontology-level continuity mechanism. Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / entities.ExposureAggregate Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml:64-71 Source finding context: ExposureAggregate.total_amount is defined as both the sum of all Exposure.amount and a month-end batch value that excludes intraday new exposures until the next batch. Source finding context: In any state after a new intraday exposure exists and before the next batch, the declared definition requires inclusion of that Exposure.amount, but the attribute note permits non-inclusion. Both claims cannot be true for the same borrower, as_of date, and exposure set. This matters because the artifact is intended as a shared authority for risk engines and reporting systems; a utilization or appetite calculation using this concept can be simultaneously 'correct' and 'incorrect' depending on which claim is applied. Source finding context: Make the temporal boundary explicit. For example, define ExposureAggregate as the sum of Exposure.amount as of a specific batch cutoff/as_of timestamp, or split it into CurrentExposureAggregate and MonthEndExposureAggregate so the universal 'all Exposure.amount' claim is not attached to a lagged stored value. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/logic.findings.yaml#logic-candidate-001 Source finding context: Shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems using exposure aggregates in risk and utilization calculations. Source finding context: A borrower receives an intraday new exposure after the month-end batch and before the next batch, while ExposureAggregate.total_amount is used as the authoritative total exposure. Source finding context: The same ontology permits two incompatible truth conditions for the aggregate, weakening review trust and making downstream calculations non-actionable without an extra unstated temporal convention. Source finding context: The aggregate concept lacks a single temporal authority: its definition uses an unqualified universal sum, while its attribute note authorizes a lagged batch projection. Source finding context: ExposureAggregate is defined as borrower-level total exposure and the sum of all Exposure.amount. Source finding context: ExposureAggregate.total_amount is stored by a month-end batch and intraday new exposures are not reflected until the next batch. Source finding context: Limit utilization is calculated from ExposureAggregate.total_amount, so the contradiction affects a declared downstream rule inside the target.
  - affected purpose: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 리스크·한도 판단 기준 및 시간 경과에 따른 risk calculations/reports의 공유 해석. Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 리스크/한도 판단 기준 제공. Source finding context: 리스크 엔진과 보고서 시스템이 공유하는 여신 리스크 개념 권위 문서 Source finding context: Shared interpretation of risk calculations and reports over time. Source finding context: Shared concept authority for risk engines and reporting systems using exposure aggregates in risk and utilization calculations.
  - failure condition: 일중 신규 여신 발생 후 다음 batch 전 한도 소진율을 계산하거나, 과거 기준일의 등급·한도·LTV·리스크 성향 준수상태를 재현하거나, FX table/batch cadence/cutoff/rule이 변경된 뒤 과거 값과 비교할 때. Source finding context: 일중 신규 여신이 발생했지만 월말 배치 전 한도 소진율이나 RiskAppetite 준수 상태가 계산될 때. Source finding context: 보고 시스템이 과거 기준일의 등급, 한도, LTV, 리스크 성향 준수상태를 재현하거나 엔진 산출값과 대사해야 하는 경우 Source finding context: FX tables, batch cadence, cutoff policy, collateral revaluation threshold, or report timing changes after records have already been produced. Source finding context: A borrower receives an intraday new exposure after the month-end batch and before the next batch, while ExposureAggregate.total_amount is used as the authoritative total exposure.
  - impact: 공식 계산이 실제 exposure보다 낮은 lagged 값을 사용할 수 있고, 같은 필드의 과거·현재 의미가 달라져 엔진/보고 대사, trend reporting, 감사 재현성이 약해진다. Source finding context: 실제 익스포저보다 낮은 집계가 공식 계산에 사용되어 한도 관리와 보고 신뢰를 동시에 약화한다. Source finding context: Historical and current values with the same ontology fields may no longer be comparable, making risk trend reporting and engine/report reconciliation unreliable.   - source lenses: axiology, coverage, evolution, logic
  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: 이 온톨로지의 선언 목적은 리스크 엔진과 보고 시스템이 공유하는 리스크·한도 판단 기준과 시간 경과에 따른 계산·보고의 공통 해석을 제공하는 것이다. 그런데 공식 한도 소진율이 일중 신규 여신을 반영하지 않는 월말 배치 집계에 의존할 수 있고, 과거 기준일의 등급·한도·LTV·준수상태 및 계산 context를 재구성할 수 없으면 엔진/보고 대사, trend reporting, 감사 재현성이 모두 약해진다. 따라서 같은 필드가 시점이나 운영 규칙 변화에 따라 다른 의미를 갖게 되어 공유 권위 문서로서의 목적을 직접 훼손한다.
  - root cause: 시작점은 ExposureAggregate와 derived risk calculations에 단일한 시간·계산 권위가 없다는 점이다. ExposureAggregate는 모든 Exposure.amount의 합으로 정의되면서도 월말 배치 저장값으로 일중 신규 여신을 다음 배치까지 제외할 수 있고, 그 값이 Limit utilization 계산에 사용된다. 여기에 RiskRating, Exposure.risk_grade/ltv, Limit.approved_amount, RiskAppetite.threshold/compliance_status도 유효기간·이력·rule version 같은 공통 시간 축을 갖지 않아 lagged aggregate와 현재/과거 risk state가 구분 없이 섞인다.
  - causal path: 원인 사슬은 네 갈래 증거가 같은 방향으로 수렴한다. 첫째, ExposureAggregate.total_amount는 all exposure sum으로 선언되지만 월말 배치 note는 일중 신규 여신 미반영을 허용하고, classification rule은 이 값을 한도 소진율 기준으로 쓴다. 둘째, ExposureAggregate.as_of와 Collateral.appraised_at 외에는 주요 risk value에 동등한 이력 속성이 없어 기준일 보고값을 온톨로지 내부 개념만으로 닫을 수 없다. 셋째, FX table, batch cadence, cutoff, collateral revaluation policy, rule version이 모델링되지 않아 운영 규칙 변경 후 과거·현재 값 비교가 흔들린다. 넷째, logic lens는 all-exposure sum과 lagged batch projection이 같은 borrower, as_of date, exposure set에서 양립할 수 없다고 확인했다.
  - action: 우선 ExposureAggregate의 temporal boundary를 명시해 특정 batch cutoff/as_of 기준의 합으로 재정의하거나, CurrentExposureAggregate와 MonthEndExposureAggregate처럼 현재 통제용 값과 보고 snapshot 값을 분리해야 한다. 그 다음 time-dependent values와 derived metrics에 as_of 또는 valid_from/valid_to, source_event_ref, batch/run id, inclusion cutoff, FX source/rate date, rule version, valuation/revaluation policy version을 부여하고 파생 필드가 사용한 calculation context를 참조하게 해야 한다. 이 순서가 필요한 이유는 한도 소진율의 권위 exposure 값을 먼저 분리해야 등급·한도·성향 준수상태와 계산 이력도 같은 기준시점에 맞춰 재현될 수 있기 때문이다.

- issue-006 (high): 보고 수동 조정, underwriter 입력, approval system 기록은 핵심 리스크 값을 생성·변경·확정하는 통제 행위인데, 현재 모델에는 actor, time, reason, before/after value, evidence URI를 담는 event/evidence entity가 없다.

Source finding context: credit-risk-ontology.yaml / controlled manual and approval actions Source finding context: Embedded Materialized Input: classification_rules allow reporting-team manual adjustment of Exposure.risk_grade; Exposure.ltv note says underwriter enters it at review time; Limit.approved_amount note says approval system records it Source finding context: 수동 조정·입력·승인 행위에 행위자·시각·근거를 담는 감사 증거 개념이 없다. Source finding context: 이 값들은 리스크 엔진 산출 또는 승인 권한을 사람이 변경하거나 확정하는 통제 지점이다. 공유 권위 문서가 값만 정의하고 통제 행위의 증거 범주를 빠뜨리면 보고서 시스템은 조정값을 볼 수 있어도 조정 주체와 근거를 같은 개념 기준으로 설명할 수 없다. Source finding context: 수동 조정과 승인/입력 행위를 별도 이벤트 엔티티로 추가한다. 최소 속성은 target_ref, actor_ref 또는 actor_id, occurred_at, reason_code/free_text, source_system, before_value, after_value, evidence_uri이며, 조정된 값은 해당 이벤트를 참조하게 한다. Source finding context: .onto/review/20260716-ea9773ff/round1/coverage.findings.yaml#coverage-candidate-002 Source finding context: 리스크 엔진 산출값과 보고 시스템 사용값을 공유 기준으로 설명하는 권위 문서 Source finding context: 보고서 팀이 엔진 등급을 수동 조정하거나 심사역/승인 시스템이 핵심 리스크 입력값을 확정한 뒤 감사·대사·이의제기 대응이 필요한 경우 Source finding context: 값의 변경 결과만 남고 행위 근거가 개념화되지 않아 엔진값과 보고값 차이를 통제된 조정으로 입증하기 어렵다. Source finding context: 값 엔티티 중심으로 범위를 잡으면서 값을 생성·변경·승인하는 통제 행위 범주가 모델에 포함되지 않았다. Source finding context: 대상 텍스트는 보고서 팀의 수동 조정, 심사역 입력, 승인 시스템 기록을 명시한다. Source finding context: entities 목록에는 해당 행위의 행위자·시각·근거·변경 전후 값을 담는 이벤트나 증거 엔티티가 없다. Source finding context: 그 결과 조정·승인된 값은 공유될 수 있지만, 통제 행위의 정당성과 감사 추적은 공유 개념 범위 밖에 남는다.
  - affected purpose: 리스크 엔진 산출값과 보고 시스템 사용값을 공유 기준으로 설명하는 권위 문서.
  - failure condition: 보고서 팀이 엔진 등급을 수동 조정하거나 심사역/승인 시스템이 핵심 리스크 입력값을 확정한 뒤 감사·대사·이의제기 대응이 필요한 경우.
  - impact: 값의 변경 결과만 남고 행위 근거가 개념화되지 않아 엔진값과 보고값 차이를 통제된 조정으로 입증하기 어렵다.
  - root hypothesis: 값 엔티티 중심 모델링이 값을 생성·변경·승인하는 control event와 audit evidence 개념을 분리해 포함하지 않았다.

  - action candidates: fix_now
  - materiality: 권위 문서의 목적은 리스크 엔진 산출값과 보고 시스템 사용값을 공유 기준으로 설명하는 것이다. 수동 조정 또는 승인·입력 이후 값만 남고 행위 근거가 모델 밖에 있으면, 보고값과 엔진값의 차이를 통제된 조정으로 입증하거나 감사·대사·이의제기에 대응하기 어렵다.
  - root cause: 문제의 시작점은 값 엔티티 중심 모델링이다. 모델이 Exposure.risk_grade, Exposure.ltv, Limit.approved_amount 같은 결과값은 다루지만, 그 값을 생성·변경·승인한 control event와 audit evidence 개념을 별도로 포함하지 않아 책임·시각·근거·변경 전후 상태가 공유 기준에 들어오지 못한다.
  - causal path: 대상 텍스트는 reporting team의 risk grade 수동 조정, underwriter의 LTV 입력, approval system의 approved amount 기록을 명시한다. 그러나 entities 목록에는 이 행위들을 target, actor, occurred_at, reason, source system, before/after value, evidence URI와 연결하는 이벤트나 증거 엔티티가 없다. 따라서 조정·승인된 값은 공유될 수 있어도, 그 값이 왜 원 엔진값과 달라졌는지와 그 정당성은 공통 개념으로 설명되지 않는다.
  - action: ManualAdjustment 및 Approval/InputEvent 같은 이벤트 엔티티를 추가하고 target_ref, actor_ref 또는 actor_id, occurred_at, reason_code/free_text, source_system, before_value, after_value, evidence_uri를 포함시켜야 한다. 조정 또는 승인된 값은 해당 이벤트를 참조하게 하여 값 자체와 통제 행위 증거가 같은 권위 모델 안에서 추적되도록 해야 한다.

- issue-007 (high): 여러 소비 시스템과 외부 wiki/CRM/FX table에 흩어진 등급, 한도, 환율 값에 대해 canonical source, precedence, fallback, reconciliation rule이 정의되지 않아 온톨로지가 충돌 시 권위 있는 값을 결정하지 못한다.
